### [1] [Equinox: Decentralized Scheduling for Hardware-Aware Orbital Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19958)
*Ansel Kaplan Erol,Divya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Equinox，一种用于地球观测卫星星座的轻量级去中心化运行时系统。它通过将电池、热裕度和队列积压等时变约束压缩为单一的状态依赖边际执行成本，实现了自适应调度。任务仅在价值超过当前成本时执行，从而实现无策略的按价值负载丢弃，并支持跨卫星任务卸载。实验表明，相比优先级调度，科学产出提升20%，图像处理吞吐量提升31%，平均电池储备高2.2倍；在高需求下执行率是静态调度的5.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星正成为执行时间关键任务的分布式边缘平台，但轨道调度面临两大核心挑战：间歇性能量收集导致能量供应不稳定，以及时间耦合问题——过早执行任务可能导致未来电池耗尽。现有调度器依赖静态优先级，缺乏自适应丢弃工作的机制，无法有效应对这些动态约束。

Method: Equinox采用了一种基于屏障函数（barrier function）的方法，将时变约束（电池电量、热裕度、队列积压）压缩为单一的状态依赖边际执行成本。该成本在接近安全限时急剧上升，同时编码瞬时压力和未来风险。此局部信号作为星座范围的协调原语：任务仅在价值超过当前成本时执行，实现按价值排序的负载丢弃；当本地成本超过邻居时，任务通过星间链路动态卸载，实现无需路由协议或全局状态的分布式负载均衡。

Result: 在基于Jetson Orin Nano物理测量的143颗卫星星座多日仿真中，Equinox相比优先级调度将科学有效产出（goodput）提升了20%，图像处理吞吐量提升了31%，同时保持平均电池储备高出2.2倍。在高需求场景下，Equinox通过优雅地丢弃工作而非在争用下崩溃，实现了静态调度5.2倍的执行率。

Conclusion: Equinox为资源受限的轨道系统提供了一种轻量级、去中心化的自适应调度方案，通过边际成本机制有效解决了间歇性能量收集和时间耦合问题，显著提升了任务执行效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: Earth-observation satellites are emerging as distributed edge platforms for time-critical tasks, yet orbital scheduling remains challenged by intermittent energy harvesting and temporal coupling where eager execution risks future battery depletion. Existing schedulers rely on static priorities and lack mechanisms to adaptively shed work. We present Equinox, a lightweight, decentralized runtime for resource-constrained orbital systems. Equinox enables adaptive scheduling by compressing time-varying constraints, including battery charge, thermal headroom, and queue backlog, into a single state-dependent marginal cost of execution. Derived from a barrier function that rises sharply near safety limits, this cost encodes both instantaneous pressure and future risk. This local signal serves as a constellation-wide coordination primitive. Tasks execute only when their value exceeds the current cost, enabling value-ordered load shedding without explicit policies. If local costs exceed a neighbor's, tasks are dynamically offloaded over inter-satellite links, achieving distributed load balancing without routing protocols or global state. We evaluate Equinox using a multi-day simulation of a 143-satellite constellation grounded in physical Jetson Orin Nano measurements. Equinox improves scientific goodput by 20% and image-processing throughput by 31% over priority-based scheduling while maintaining 2.2x higher mean battery reserves. Under high demand, Equinox achieves 5.2x the execution rate of static scheduling by gracefully shedding work rather than collapsing under contention.

</details>


### [2] [LEO: Tracing GPU Stall Root Causes via Cross-Vendor Backward Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20032)
*Yuning Xia,John Mellor-Crummey*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LEO工具，用于诊断NVIDIA、AMD和Intel GPU上的性能停顿根因，指导优化实现1.73-1.82倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU性能分析工具存在诊断缺口：性能分析器仅能显示发生停顿的源代码行，但无法解释停顿产生的根本原因。此外，同一内核在不同GPU架构上可能表现出不同的停顿模式和成因，开发者缺乏有效的跨平台根因分析手段。

Method: 提出LEO根因分析器，采用后向切片技术从停顿指令回溯，综合考虑寄存器依赖关系以及厂商特定的同步机制，将GPU停顿归因到源代码指令，从而解释性能低效的根本原因。

Result: 在三个GPU平台的21个工作负载上验证，LEO指导的优化实现几何平均1.73倍至1.82倍的加速。案例研究表明：同一内核在不同GPU架构上需要不同的优化策略；LEO的结构化诊断信息相比仅提供代码或原始停顿计数基线，能显著提升大语言模型的代码优化效果。

Conclusion: LEO填补了GPU性能诊断的关键缺口，通过提供跨平台的停顿根因分析，帮助开发者理解不同GPU架构的性能差异，并为自动化优化工具（如大语言模型）提供有效的结构化诊断信息，显著提升优化效率。

Abstract: More than half of the Top 500 supercomputers employ GPUs as accelerators. On GPU-accelerated platforms, developers face a key diagnostic gap: profilers show source lines where stalls occur, but not why they occur. Furthermore, the same kernel may have different stalls and underlying causes on different GPUs. This paper presents LEO, a root-cause analyzer for NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel GPUs that performs backward slicing from stalled instructions, considering dependencies arising from registers as well as vendor-specific synchronization mechanisms. LEO attributes GPU stalls to source instructions with the goal of explaining root causes of these inefficiencies. Across 21 workloads on three GPU platforms, LEO-guided optimizations deliver geometric-mean speedups of 1.73$\times$--1.82$\times$. Our case studies show that (1) the same kernel may require different optimizations for different GPU architectures, and (2) LEO's structured diagnostics improve code optimization with large language models relative to code-only and raw-stall-count baselines.

</details>


### [3] [Characterizing and Fixing Silent Data Loss in Spark-on-AWS-Lambda with Open Table Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20081)
*Srujan Kumar Gandla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AWS Lambda超时终止会导致Spark-on-AWS-Lambda写入操作出现静默数据丢失。作者通过860次实验验证了该问题，并提出SafeWriter工具实现安全回滚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AWS Lambda在函数超时时会发送不可捕获的SIGKILL信号终止容器。当Spark-on-AWS-Lambda作业在写入操作的Phase 1（数据上传）和Phase 2（元数据提交）之间被终止时，会产生静默数据丢失：孤立的Parquet文件在S3上累积，表的提交状态保持不变，且标准监控不会发出警报。

Method: 1. 通过860次受控kill注入实验，在三种数据集规模下对Delta Lake和Apache Iceberg进行漏洞特征刻画。
2. 提出SafeWriter，一个语言级包装器，在Lambda超时前30秒启动看门狗线程，通过SQL触发格式原生回滚，并在S3上记录检查点文档。

Result: SIGKILL落在阶段间隙时，两种格式在100%的试验中产生静默数据丢失。SafeWriter将每个测试的kill场景转换为干净、可检测的回滚，正常写入路径仅增加不到100毫秒的开销。

Conclusion: SafeWriter有效解决了Spark-on-AWS-Lambda在Lambda超时终止场景下的静默数据丢失问题，以极低的性能开销实现了可检测的安全回滚机制。

Abstract: AWS Lambda terminates containers with an uncatchable SIGKILL signal when a function exceeds its configured timeout. When a Spark-on-AWS-Lambda (SoAL) job is killed between Phase 1 (data upload) and Phase 2 (metadata commit) of a write, the result is silent data loss: orphaned Parquet files accumulate on S3 while the table's committed state remains unchanged and standard monitoring raises no alert. We characterize this vulnerability across Delta Lake and Apache Iceberg through 860 controlled kill-injection experiments at three dataset sizes. A SIGKILL landing in the inter-phase gap produced silent data loss in 100% of trials for both formats. We then present SafeWriter, a language-level wrapper that arms a watchdog thread 30 seconds before the Lambda timeout, triggers a format-native rollback via SQL, and records a checkpoint document on S3. SafeWriter converted every tested kill scenario into a clean, detectable rollback with under 100 ms added to normal write paths.

</details>


### [4] [e112: A Context-Aware Mobile Emergency Communication Platform Leveraging Smartphone Sensing and Cloud Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20342)
*Katerina Ioannidou,Marios D. Dikaiakos,Athena Stassopoulou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了e112，一款情境感知的移动应急响应应用，旨在加强灾害期间公民与当局之间的沟通，评估显示其具有高用户满意度和稳健性能，能显著提升应急准备与响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在气候变化引发的紧急情况日益频繁的背景下，利用智能手机的普及性来加强公民与当局之间的沟通，以降低灾害期间对人类生命的风险。

Method: 采用以用户为中心的设计方法进行开发，构建了基于云的后端和操作员仪表盘以支持态势感知。系统提供SOS请求、事件报告、定制警报、疏散引导和 moderated 社区互动功能，并通过可用性研究和技术审计进行评估。

Result: 可用性研究和技术审计表明，该应用具有高用户满意度、稳健的性能和良好的可访问性，能够在压力环境下保持清晰度和可用性。

Conclusion: 设计良好且简单的移动应用程序可以显著增强应急准备和响应能力，从而减少气候变化引发的紧急情况对人类生命的风险。

Abstract: This paper presents e112, a context-aware mobile emergency response application designed to strengthen communication between citizens and authorities during disasters. Building on the ubiquity of smartphones, the system provides SOS requests, incident reporting, customized alerts, evacuation guidance, and moderated community interaction, supported by a cloud-based back end and an operator dashboard for situational awareness. A user-centered design approach guided our development, ensuring clarity and usability under stressful conditions. Evaluation through usability studies and technical audits demonstrated high user satisfaction, robust performance, and accessibility. The results show that a simple, well-designed mobile application can significantly enhance emergency preparedness and response, reducing risks to human life during climate change--driven emergencies.

</details>


### [5] [Extending Contract Verification for Parallel Programming Models to Fortran](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20410)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将基于契约的并行程序验证框架CoVer扩展至Fortran语言，实现了跨语言（C/C++和Fortran）的静态与动态分析，分析效率显著优于现有工具MUST，并发现了MPI-BugBench中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MPI等并行编程模型的正确性检查工具通常局限于C/C++代码、绑定特定库实现或仅能检测特定类型的错误。为了克服这些局限性，需要开发一种通用、跨语言的验证框架。

Method: 在已有CoVer框架基础上，针对Fortran语言特性调整了契约定义，并修改了分析算法以同时支持C/C++和Fortran程序的静态与动态分析。

Result: 扩展后的CoVer框架在保持分析准确性的同时，分析效率显著优于最先进的工具MUST；在实际测试中发现了MPI-BugBench测试框架中的一个bug，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功实现了并行程序验证框架的跨语言支持，证明了基于契约的通用验证方法在多语言环境下的可行性与高效性，为并行程序正确性检测提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: High-performance computing often relies on parallel programming models such as MPI for distributed-memory systems. While powerful, these models are prone to subtle programming errors, leading to development of multiple correctness checking tools. However, these are often limited to C/C++ codes, tied to specific library implementations, or restricted to certain error classes. Building on our prior work with CoVer, a generic, contract-based verification framework for parallel programming models, we extend CoVer's applicability to Fortran, enabling static and dynamic analysis across multiple programming languages. We adapted language-specific contract definitions and modified the analyses to support both C/C++ and Fortran programs. Our evaluation demonstrates that the enhanced version preserves CoVer's analysis accuracy and even revealed a bug in the MPI-BugBench testing framework, underscoring the effectiveness of the approach. The Fortran port of CoVer turns out to be substantially more efficient than the state-of-the-art tool MUST, while maintaining generality across languages.

</details>


### [6] [FASER: Fine-Grained Phase Management for Speculative Decoding in Dynamic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20503)
*Wenyan Chen,Chengzhi Lu,Yanying Lin,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FASER系统，通过细粒度的推测解码阶段管理，动态调整推测长度、提前剪枝拒绝token，并将验证阶段分块与草稿阶段重叠执行，从而提升LLM推理吞吐量高达53%，降低延迟高达1.92倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码系统采用粗粒度管理方式，无法适应动态变化的在线推理流量：低负载时草稿阶段阻塞验证阶段导致GPU资源利用率不足和延迟增加；高负载时在验证阶段浪费计算资源处理被拒绝的token，导致GPU资源过载。

Method: FASER采用细粒度推测解码阶段管理：1）动态调整连续批处理中每个请求的推测长度，并在验证阶段内对被拒绝token进行提前剪枝以最小化计算浪费；2）将验证阶段分解为多个frontier块，通过细粒度空间复用以最小资源干扰实现与草稿阶段的流水线重叠执行。

Result: 在vLLM中实现的FASER原型相比最先进系统，吞吐量提升高达53%，延迟降低高达1.92倍。

Conclusion: FASER通过细粒度阶段管理成功解决了现有推测解码系统在动态在线推理流量下的适应性问题，在保证资源高效利用的同时显著提升了系统性能，为LLM推理加速提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) is a widely used approach for accelerating decode-heavy LLM inference workloads. While online inference workloads are highly dynamic, existing SD systems are rigid and take a coarse-grained approach to SD management. They typically set the speculative token length for an entire batch and serialize the execution of the draft and verification phases. Consequently, these systems fall short at adapting to volatile online inference traffic. Under low load, they exhibit prolonged latency because the draft phase blocks the verification phase for the entire batch, leaving GPU computing resources underutilized. Conversely, under high load, they waste computation on rejected tokens during the verification phase, overloading GPU resources.
  We introduce FASER, a novel system that features fine-grained SD phase management. First, FASER minimizes computational waste by dynamically adjusting the speculative length for each request within a continuous batch and by performing early pruning of rejected tokens inside the verification phase. Second, FASER breaks the verification phase into frontiers, or chunks, to overlap them with the draft phase. This overlap is achieved via fine-grained spatial multiplexing with minimal resource interference. Our FASER prototype in vLLM improves throughput by up to 53% and reduces latency by up to 1.92$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art systems.

</details>

### [7] [ChipCraftBrain: Validation-First RTL Generation via Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19856)
*Cagri Eryilmaz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ChipCraftBrain框架通过符号-神经混合推理与自适应多智能体协同，将RTL生成准确率提升至97.2%（VerilogEval）和94.7%（CVDP），显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM单次生成RTL代码功能正确率仅60-65%，多智能体方法虽达95.9%但未在工业级基准测试验证、缺乏综合感知且API成本高。

Method: 提出四大创新：(1)基于PPO策略的自适应六智能体编排（168维状态空间）；(2)符号-神经混合架构，算法求解K-map和真值表问题；(3)321个模式库+971个开源参考的知识增强生成；(4)层次化规格分解为依赖序子模块。

Result: VerilogEval-Human达97.2% pass@1（最优154/156）；CVDP 302问题子集达94.7%（较基线提升36-60个百分点）；RISC-V SoC案例成功生成8个模块（689行代码）并通过FPGA验证。

Conclusion: ChipCraftBrain在标准与工业级基准上均达到SOTA性能，以约30倍更少的尝试次数超越NVIDIA ACE-RTL，验证了符号-神经混合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for generating Register-Transfer Level (RTL) code from natural language specifications, but single-shot generation achieves only 60-65% functional correctness on standard benchmarks. Multi-agent approaches such as MAGE reach 95.9% on VerilogEval yet remain untested on harder industrial benchmarks such as NVIDIA's CVDP, lack synthesis awareness, and incur high API costs.
  We present ChipCraftBrain, a framework combining symbolic-neural reasoning with adaptive multi-agent orchestration for automated RTL generation. Four innovations drive the system: (1) adaptive orchestration over six specialized agents via a PPO policy over a 168-dim state (an alternative world-model MPC planner is also evaluated); (2) a hybrid symbolic-neural architecture that solves K-map and truth-table problems algorithmically while specialized agents handle waveform timing and general RTL; (3) knowledge-augmented generation from a 321-pattern base plus 971 open-source reference implementations with focus-aware retrieval; and (4) hierarchical specification decomposition into dependency-ordered sub-modules with interface synchronization.
  On VerilogEval-Human, ChipCraftBrain achieves 97.2% mean pass@1 (range 96.15-98.72% across 7 runs, best 154/156), on par with ChipAgents (97.4%, self-reported) and ahead of MAGE (95.9%). On a 302-problem non-agentic subset of CVDP spanning five task categories, we reach 94.7% mean pass@1 (286/302, averaged over 3 runs), a 36-60 percentage-point lift per category over the published single-shot baseline; we additionally lead three of four categories shared with NVIDIA's ACE-RTL despite using roughly 30x fewer per-problem attempts. A RISC-V SoC case study demonstrates hierarchical decomposition generating 8/8 lint-passing modules (689 LOC) validated on FPGA, where monolithic generation fails entirely.

</details>


### [8] [Efficient Page Migration in Hybrid Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19932)
*Upasna,Venkata Kalyan Tavva*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Duon机制，通过扩展TLB和页表直接存储迁移后的映射信息，消除异构内存页面迁移时的TLB清降和缓存行失效开销，IPC提升3.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扁平地址空间的异构内存架构中，将热页从慢速内存迁移到快速内存时，需要更新TLB映射（TLB shootdown）和使缓存行失效，这会带来显著的性能开销。

Method: 提出Duon方案，利用扩展TLB和页表，将重新映射页面的更新映射信息直接存储在TLB和页表自身中，从而无需在页面迁移后执行TLB shootdown和缓存行失效操作。该方案可与任意现有页面迁移策略协同工作。

Result: 相比现有最先进技术，Duon实现了平均3.87%的IPC提升，有效降低了异构内存系统中页面迁移的开销。

Conclusion: Duon成功解决了扁平地址空间异构内存系统中页面迁移的性能瓶颈，能够在不改变迁移策略的前提下显著提升系统性能，为异构内存优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Memory Architecture (HMA) aims to optimize memory usage by leveraging a combination of memory types, such as high-bandwidth memory (HBM), commodity DRAM, and non-volatile memory (NVM), when utilized as main memory. To achieve maximum performance benefits, frequently accessed data pages are prioritized for storage in the faster HBM, while less frequently accessed pages are stored in slower memory types like DRAM or NVM. This enables a more efficient allocation of memory resources and improves overall system performance. In a Flat Address Space memory organization, all memory types, both fast and slow, are treated as a unified memory pool. This approach increases the overall memory capacity accessible to the system. In Flat Address Space organization, frequently accessed data pages may need to be remapped from slower memory to faster memory to improve memory access times. Such relocation requires changes to the data/states in the TLB (TLB shootdown) and the processor cache (cache line invalidations), leading to performance degradation. To address these inefficiencies, we propose a novel solution called Duon. The goal of Duon is to eliminate the overheads associated with page migration in systems using Extended TLB and Page Table. Specifically, our approach ensures that the updated mapping information for remapped pages is carefully stored directly in the TLB and page table itself. By doing so, the need for TLB shootdown and cache line invalidation after page migration is eliminated. Consequently, our proposal results in an overall improvement in IPC by 3.87% over existing state-of-the-art techniques, enhancing the efficiency and performance of heterogeneous memory systems. Further, our approach can work with any of the existing page migration policies and improve the performance.

</details>


### [9] [Algorithm and Hardware Co-Design for Efficient Complex-Valued Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19993)
*Zehuan Zhang,Mark Chen,He Li,Wayne Luk*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了基于Dropout的贝叶斯复值神经网络（BayesCVNNs），实现了复值应用的不确定性量化，并提供了自动化配置搜索方法和FPGA加速器框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的复值神经网络（CVNNs）无法量化预测不确定性，而复值的双部分特性又极大地扩展了设计空间，使得人工设计最优配置变得困难。

Method: 提出基于Dropout的贝叶斯复值神经网络（BayesCVNNs）；引入基于层混合和部分混合的自动化搜索方法以识别最优配置；开发定制化FPGA加速器生成框架，利用优化的构建模块进行硬件部署。

Result: 自动化搜索能有效找到最佳配置，性能优于手工设计模型且硬件成本更低；优化的FPGA加速器在不同模型上实现约4.5倍和13倍加速，功耗低于GPU实现的10%，在算法和硬件方面均超越现有工作。

Conclusion: BayesCVNNs成功填补了复值神经网络不确定性量化的空白，结合自动化搜索与FPGA加速，实现了高效、低功耗且高性能的复值神经网络部署方案。

Abstract: Complex-Valued Neural Networks (CVNNs) have significant advantages in handling tasks that involve complex numbers. However, existing CVNNs are unable to quantify predictive uncertainty. We propose, for the first time, dropout-based Bayesian Complex-Valued Neural Networks (BayesCVNNs) to enable uncertainty quantification for complex-valued applications, exhibiting broad applicability and efficiency for hardware implementation due to modularity. Furthermore, as the dual-part nature of complex values significantly broadens the design space and enables novel configurations based on layer-mixing and part-mixing, we introduce an automated search approach to effectively identify optimal configurations for both real and imaginary components. To facilitate deployment, we present a framework that generates customized FPGA-based accelerators for BayesCVNNs, leveraging a set of optimized building blocks. Experiments demonstrate the best configuration can be effectively found via the automated search, attaining higher performance with lower hardware costs compared with manually crafted models. The optimized accelerators achieve approximately 4.5x and 13x speedups on different models with less than 10% power consumption compared to GPU implementations, and outperform existing work in both algorithm and hardware aspects. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/zehuanzhang/BayesCVNN.git.

</details>


### [10] [EnergAIzer: Fast and Accurate GPU Power Estimation Framework for AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20105)
*Kyungmi Lee,Zhiye Song,Eun Kyung Lee,Xin Zhang,Tamar Eilam,Anantha P. Chandrakasan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出EnergAIzer，一种轻量级GPU功耗预测工具，通过分析AI负载的结构化模式来预测硬件利用率，将预测时间从数小时缩短至秒级，在NVIDIA Ampere GPU上实现8%的功耗误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工作负载推动数据中心功耗增加，准确的GPU功耗估计对于主动功耗管理至关重要。然而现有功耗模型面临可扩展性瓶颈——不在于建模技术本身，而在于获取其所需的硬件利用率输入。传统方法依赖昂贵的仿真或硬件性能分析，在需要快速预测时不切实际。

Method: 基于AI负载中的核函数常采用产生结构化模式的优化这一关键洞察，这些模式可解析地确定内存流量和执行时间线。利用这些模式作为解析支架构建性能模型进行数据拟合，自然地暴露模块级利用率，然后将预测的利用率输入功耗模型以估计动态功耗。

Result: 在NVIDIA Ampere GPU上实现8%的功耗误差，与传统使用精细周期级仿真或硬件性能分析的功耗模型具有竞争力；展示了在频率扩展和架构配置方面的探索能力，包括仅用7%误差预测NVIDIA H100功耗。

Conclusion: EnergAIzer为AI负载提供快速准确的功耗预测，为功耗感知的设计探索铺平了道路，解决了现有方法在可扩展性方面的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: As AI workloads drive increases in datacenter power consumption, accurate GPU power estimation is critical for proactive power management. However, existing power models face a scalability bottleneck not in the modeling techniques themselves, but in obtaining the hardware utilization inputs they require. Conventional approaches rely on either costly simulation or hardware profiling, which makes them impractical when rapid predictions are required.
  This work presents EnergAIzer, which addresses this scalability bottleneck by developing a lightweight solution to predict utilization inputs, reducing the estimation walltime from hours to seconds. Our key insight is that kernels in AI workloads commonly employ optimizations that create structured patterns, which analytically determine memory traffic and execution timeline. We construct a performance model using these patterns as an analytical scaffold for empirical data fitting, which also naturally exposes module-level utilization. This predicted utilization is then fed into our power model to estimate dynamic power consumption.
  EnergAIzer achieves 8% power errors on NVIDIA Ampere GPUs, competitive with traditional power models with elaborate cycle-level simulation or hardware profiling. We demonstrate EnergAIzer's exploration capabilities for frequency scaling and architectural configurations, including forecasting the power of NVIDIA H100 with just 7% error. In summary, EnergAIzer provides fast and accurate power prediction for AI workloads, paving the way for power-aware design explorations.

</details>


### [11] [A Novel Low-Power Cache Architecture Based on 6-Transistor SRAM Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20176)
*Naser Khatti Dizabadi,Ceyda Elcin Kaya*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于传统6T SRAM单元串联连接的低功耗缓存架构，在不增加单元晶体管数量的前提下，通过堆叠效应显著降低漏电功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着集成电路工艺的进步，SRAM缓存中的漏电功耗问题日益严重。传统方法往往通过增加单元晶体管数量或改变工艺来抑制漏电，但这会带来面积开销或制造难度。本研究旨在探索一种无需改变标准6T SRAM单元本身即可降低漏电功耗的架构级方案。

Method: 提出了一种串联互联的缓存架构，将同一列中的相邻SRAM单元重新配置为串联拓扑结构。该设计利用晶体管堆叠效应在保持操作期间抑制漏电流。为适配此架构，相应地修改了地址译码和读出电路，包括列组织和读出路径的调整。使用Keysight ADS工具进行瞬态仿真以验证方案有效性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统的SRAM并行互联方案相比，所提出的串联架构在保持标准6T SRAM单元不变的情况下，有效降低了漏电功耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于串联互联的缓存架构成功实现了漏电功耗的降低，证明了在不增加存储单元复杂度的前提下，通过架构层面的创新解决漏电问题的可行性，为低功耗SRAM缓存设计提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-power cache architecture based on the series interconnection of conventional 6-transistor static random-access memory (6T SRAM) cells. The proposed approach aims to reduce leakage power in SRAM-based cache memories without increasing the transistor count of the memory cell itself. In the proposed architecture, adjacent cells within a column are reconfigured in a serial topology, thereby exploiting the stacking effect to suppress leakage current, particularly during hold operation. This architectural modification requires corresponding changes to the addressing and sensing structure of the cache, including adjustments to the column organization and readout path. To evaluate the proposed method, transient simulations were carried out using Keysight ADS. The simulation results show that the proposed architecture reduces leakage power compared with the conventional SRAM interconnection scheme while preserving the use of standard 6T SRAM cells.

</details>


### [12] [Evaluating Computing Platforms for Sustainability: A Comparative Analysis of FPGAs against ASICs, GPUs, and CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20638)
*Chetan Choppali Sudarshan,Aman Arora,Vidya A Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenFPGA工具，用于估算FPGA全生命周期的碳足迹，发现FPGA在频繁变更的多样化工作负载和低产量应用场景下比ASIC、GPU和CPU更具可持续性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着气候变化问题日益严峻，可持续计算变得至关重要。由于FPGA具有可重配置性，能够在多种应用中分摊硬件制造产生的碳足迹，因此被视为具有潜力的可持续计算平台。然而，目前缺乏全面评估FPGA全生命周期碳足迹的工具和方法。

Method: 开发了GreenFPGA工具，用于估算FPGA全生命周期的总碳足迹，考虑了碳足迹建模中的不确定性因素。该工具涵盖了设计、制造、重配置（重用）、运行、处置、测试和回收等各阶段的碳足迹，并在等性能假设条件下比较FPGA与ASIC、GPU、CPU的可持续性。

Result: 实验结果表明，在等性能假设下，FPGA在特定部署场景（频繁变更的多样化工作负载和低产量应用）中比ASIC、GPU、CPU更具可持续性。研究强调了需要跨不同计算平台分析应用碳足迹的重要性，应用类型、使用寿命、使用时间和产量等参数会影响总体碳足迹。

Conclusion: FPGA在特定部署场景下可以作为比ASIC、GPU、CPU更可持续的计算平台选择，特别是对于工作负载频繁变化、应用多样性高且产量较低的场景。GreenFPGA工具为评估和优化FPGA的碳足迹提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Climate change concerns emphasize the need for sustainable computing. Modeling the carbon footprint (CFP), including operational and embodied CFP from semiconductor use, manufacture and design, is essential. Field programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) stand out as promising platforms due to their reconfigurability across various applications, enabling the amortization of embodied CFP across multiple applications. This paper introduces GreenFPGA, a tool estimating the total CFP of FPGAs over their lifespan, considering uncertainties in CFP modeling. It accounts for CFP during design, manufacturing, reconfigurability (reuse), operation, disposal, testing, and recycling. GreenFPGA identifies deployment regimes in which FPGAs can be more sustainable than ASICs, GPUs, and CPUs under the modeled iso-performance assumptions. Experimental results highlight the importance of analyzing applications across different computing platforms to assess their CFP while varying parameters such as application type, lifetime, usage time, and volume impact their total CFP. Across the evaluated pairwise iso-performance case studies with ASICs, GPUs, and CPUs, FPGAs can be more sustainable under specific deployment regimes involving frequently changing, diverse workloads and low-volume applications.

</details>

### [13] [WorkflowGen:an adaptive workflow generation mechanism driven by trajectory experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19756)
*Ruocan Wei,Shufeng Wang,Ziwei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WorkflowGen框架，通过轨迹经验驱动和自适应路由策略，自动生成工作流，在无需标注数据下降低token消耗40%以上，成功率提升20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）代理在处理业务查询、工具调用和工作流编排等复杂任务时，存在推理开销大、token消耗高、执行不稳定以及无法复用历史经验的问题。传统方法为每个查询从头生成工作流，导致成本高、响应慢且鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出WorkflowGen框架，采用轨迹经验驱动的自适应方法。在执行初期捕获完整轨迹，提取节点和工作流两级可复用知识（包括错误指纹、最优工具映射、参数模式、执行路径和异常规避策略）。引入闭环机制，仅对可变节点通过轨迹重写、经验更新和模板归纳进行轻量级生成。设计三层自适应路由策略，根据与历史查询的语义相似度，动态选择直接复用、基于重写的生成或完全初始化。

Result: 在无需大规模标注数据的情况下，与实时规划方法相比，token消耗降低超过40%；在中等相似度查询上，通过主动错误规避和自适应回退，成功率提升20%；通过模块化、可追溯的经验和跨场景适应性增强了可部署性。

Conclusion: WorkflowGen在效率、鲁棒性和可解释性之间取得了实际平衡，有效解决了现有方法的关键局限性，为复杂任务中的LLM代理工作流生成提供了高效、稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents often suffer from high reasoning overhead, excessive token consumption, unstable execution, and inability to reuse past experiences in complex tasks like business queries, tool use, and workflow orchestration. Traditional methods generate workflows from scratch for every query, leading to high cost, slow response, and poor robustness. We propose WorkflowGen, an adaptive, trajectory experience-driven framework for automatic workflow generation that reduces token usage and improves efficiency and success rate. Early in execution, WorkflowGen captures full trajectories and extracts reusable knowledge at both node and workflow levels, including error fingerprints, optimal tool mappings, parameter schemas, execution paths, and exception-avoidance strategies. It then employs a closed-loop mechanism that performs lightweight generation only on variable nodes via trajectory rewriting, experience updating, and template induction. A three-tier adaptive routing strategy dynamically selects among direct reuse, rewriting-based generation, and full initialization based on semantic similarity to historical queries. Without large annotated datasets, we qualitatively compare WorkflowGen against real-time planning, static single trajectory, and basic in-context learning baselines. Our method reduces token consumption by over 40 percent compared to real-time planning, improves success rate by 20 percent on medium-similarity queries through proactive error avoidance and adaptive fallback, and enhances deployability via modular, traceable experiences and cross-scenario adaptability. WorkflowGen achieves a practical balance of efficiency, robustness, and interpretability, addressing key limitations of existing approaches.

</details>


### [14] [Transparent Screening for LLM Inference and Training Impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19757)
*Arnault Pachot,Thierry Petit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个透明筛选框架，用于在有限可观测性条件下估算大语言模型的训练与推理影响，通过将自然语言应用描述转化为有界环境估算，支持市场模型的可比性在线观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于当前大型语言模型（尤其是专有服务）的不透明性和有限的可观测性，直接测量其环境影响存在困难，缺乏可比较、透明和可复现的数据。因此，需要一个能够在有限信息下提供可审计、可比较的代理估算方法。

Method: 提出了一个透明筛选框架，该框架将自然语言形式的应用描述转化为有界的环境影响估算值。该方法不声称对不透明的专有服务进行直接测量，而是提供一种可审计、与源数据链接的代理方法论。

Result: 该框架实现了对当前市场模型环境影响的估算，并支持一个用于比较当前市场模型的在线观测平台，提高了不同模型之间影响的可比性。

Conclusion: 通过提供可审计、来源关联的代理方法，该框架增强了对大语言模型环境影响评估的透明度、可比性和可复现性，为在有限可观测性下的影响评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a transparent screening framework for estimating inference and training impacts of current large language models under limited observability. The framework converts natural-language application descriptions into bounded environmental estimates and supports a comparative online observatory of current market models. Rather than claiming direct measurement for opaque proprietary services, it provides an auditable, source-linked proxy methodology designed to improve comparability, transparency, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [15] [Accelerating PayPal's Commerce Agent with Speculative Decoding: An Empirical Study on EAGLE3 with Fine-Tuned Nemotron Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19767)
*Ally Qin,Jian Wan,Sarat Mudunuri,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在PayPal Commerce Agent（基于llama3.1-nemotron-nano-8B-v1）上评估EAGLE3投机解码，实现了高达49%的吞吐量提升、33%的延迟降低及50%的GPU成本节省，同时保持了输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前NEMO-4-PAYPAL工作通过领域微调降低延迟和成本的基础上，进一步探索推理时优化技术（投机解码），以在不增加硬件成本的前提下提升PayPal Commerce Agent的推理性能。

Method: 使用vLLM实现EAGLE3投机解码，在2xH100硬件上与NVIDIA NIM进行对比基准测试。实验覆盖40种配置，包括不同的投机token数量（gamma=3, 5）、并发级别（1-32）和采样温度（0, 0.5）。

Result: 主要发现：（1）gamma=3在零额外硬件成本下实现22-49%吞吐量提升和18-33%延迟降低；（2）gamma=3的接受率在所有条件下稳定在约35.5%；（3）gamma=5收益递减（接受率约25%）；（4）LLM-as-Judge评估确认输出质量完全保持；（5）单卡H100投机解码性能匹配或超越双卡H100 NIM，实现50% GPU成本削减。

Conclusion: EAGLE3投机解码是LLM推理的高效优化手段，能显著提升吞吐量、降低延迟与成本，且不损害输出质量，为工业部署提供了高性价比方案。

Abstract: We evaluate speculative decoding with EAGLE3 as an inference-time optimization for PayPal's Commerce Agent, powered by a fine-tuned llama3.1-nemotron-nano-8B-v1 model. Building on prior work (NEMO-4-PAYPAL) that reduced latency and cost through domain-specific fine-tuning, we benchmark EAGLE3 via vLLM against NVIDIA NIM on identical 2xH100 hardware across 40 configurations spanning speculative token counts (gamma=3, gamma=5), concurrency levels (1-32), and sampling temperatures (0, 0.5). Key findings: (1) gamma=3 achieves 22-49% throughput improvement and 18-33% latency reduction at zero additional hardware cost; (2) acceptance rates remain stable at approximately 35.5% for gamma=3 across all conditions; (3) gamma=5 yields diminishing returns (approximately 25% acceptance rate); (4) LLM-as-Judge evaluation confirms fully preserved output quality; and (5) speculative decoding on a single H100 matches or exceeds NIM on two H100s, enabling 50% GPU cost reduction.

</details>


### [16] [On-Meter Graph Machine Learning: A Case Study of PV Power Forecasting for Grid Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19800)
*Jian Huang,Zixiang Ming,Yongli Zhu,Linna Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在微电网边缘智能电表上部署图神经网络（GCN和GraphSAGE）以预测光伏发电量，成功在智能电表上实现了模型部署和运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在微电网环境中实现光伏发电量的边缘预测，探索将图神经网络模型部署到资源受限的边缘智能电表上的可行性与方法。

Method: 采用了GCN和GraphSAGE两种图机器学习模型，利用ONNX和ONNX Runtime技术，重点开发并部署了针对GCN的定制化ONNX算子，将模型部署在智能电表硬件上。

Result: 基于真实村庄微电网数据集进行了案例研究，两种模型在PC端和智能电表上均成功部署并运行，论文对比了它们在两种平台上的性能表现。

Conclusion: 证明了通过ONNX等技术优化，可以将图神经网络成功部署于边缘智能电表进行光伏功率预测，验证了边缘计算在该场景下的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a detailed study of how graph neural networks can be used on edge intelligent meters in a microgrid to forecast photovoltaic power generation. The problem background and the adopted technologies are introduced, including ONNX and ONNX Runtime. The hardware and software specifications of the smart meter are also briefly described. Then, the paper focuses on the training and deployment of two graph machine learning models, GCN and GraphSAGE, with particular emphasis on developing and deploying a customized ONNX operator for GCN. Finally, a case study is conducted using real datasets from a village microgrid. The performance of the two models is compared on both the PC and the smart meter, exhibiting successful deployments and executions on the smart meter.

</details>


### [17] [Expert Upcycling: Shifting the Compute-Efficient Frontier of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19835)
*Chaitanya Dwivedi,Binxuan Huang,Himanshu Gupta,Pratik Jayarao,Neeraj Varshney,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家升级方法，在持续预训练中动态扩展MoE专家数量，相比从头训练大模型节省32% GPU时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型通过增加专家数量提升性能，但训练大型MoE内存和通信成本随总参数规模增长，训练开销巨大；现有方法需从头训练，资源效率低。

Method: 提出专家升级方法：将训练好的E专家模型通过专家复制和路由器扩展构建mE专家模型（m>E），保持top-K路由不变；引入基于效用的专家选择，利用梯度重要性分数指导非均匀复制；后续持续预训练打破复制专家对称性实现专业化。

Result: 在7B-13B参数实验中，升级模型验证损失达到固定规模基线水平，节省32% GPU时间；基于效用的选择在有限CPT下将差距闭合提升三倍；理论框架将质量差距分解为容量项和初始化项。

Conclusion: 专家升级是一种原则性、计算高效的替代方案，可用于部署大型MoE模型，为MoE扩展提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for scaling large language models: frontier models routinely decouple total parameters from per-token computation through sparse expert routing. Scaling laws show that under fixed active computation, model quality scales predictably with total parameters, and MoEs realize this by increasing expert count. However, training large MoEs is expensive, as memory requirements and inter-device communication both scale with total parameter count. We propose expert upcycling, a method for progressively expanding MoE capacity by increasing the number of experts during continued pre-training (CPT). Given a trained E-expert model, the upcycling operator constructs an mE-expert model through expert duplication and router extension while holding top-K routing fixed, preserving per-token inference cost. Duplication provides a warm initialization: the expanded model inherits the source checkpoint's learned representations, starting from a substantially lower loss than random initialization. Subsequent CPT then breaks the symmetry among duplicated experts to drive specialization. We formalize the upcycling operator and develop a theoretical framework decomposing the quality gap into a capacity term and an initialization term. We further introduce utility-based expert selection, which uses gradient-based importance scores to guide non-uniform duplication, more than tripling gap closure when CPT is limited. In our 7B-13B total parameter experiments, the upcycled model matches the fixed-size baseline on validation loss while saving 32% of GPU hours. Comprehensive ablations across model scales, activation ratios, MoE architectures, and training budgets yield a practical recipe for deploying expert upcycling, establishing it as a principled, compute-efficient alternative to training large MoE models from scratch.

</details>


### [18] [Graph-Theoretic Models for the Prediction of Molecular Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19840)
*Anna Niane,Prudence Djagba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于图论的分子性质预测模型泛化能力不足的问题，提出系统增强框架，将平均R²从0.24提升至0.79，在无GPU条件下匹配或超越深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mukwembi和Nyabadza提出的基于外部活性指数D(G)和内部活性指数ζ(G)的模型在小规模黄酮类数据集上表现良好，但其在更大规模、化学多样性数据集上的泛化能力尚未得到验证。

Method: 在五个MoleculeNet基准数据集上评估基线D(G)-ζ(G)多项式模型，并提出系统增强框架：逐步引入Ridge正则化、额外图描述符、物理化学性质、梯度提升集成学习、Lasso特征选择，以及结合拓扑指数与Morgan指纹的混合方法。

Result: 增强模型将平均最佳R²从基线的0.24提升至0.79，单个数据集提升幅度达165%-274%，所有改进均具有统计显著性(p<0.001)。在相同实验条件下，增强后的经典模型在所有五个数据集上匹配或超越图卷积网络，并与近期GNN+PGM混合模型表现相当。整个框架无需GPU，训练时间少于五分钟。

Conclusion: 经过系统增强的经典图论模型能够有效泛化到大规模化学多样性数据集，性能可媲美甚至超越深度学习方法，同时具备计算成本低、无需GPU、开源工具实现等优势，适合资源受限环境下的研究应用。

Abstract: Graph-theoretic approaches offer simplicity, interpretability, and low computational cost for molecular property prediction. Among these, the model proposed by Mukwembi and Nyabadza, based on the external activity $D(G)$ and internal activity $ζ(G)$ indices, achieved strong results on a small flavonoid dataset. However, its ability to generalize to larger and chemically diverse datasets has not been tested. This study evaluates the baseline $D(G)$-$ζ(G)$ polynomial model on five benchmark datasets from MoleculeNet, covering biological activity (BACE, 1,513 molecules), lipophilicity (LogP synthetic, 14,610 molecules; LogP experimental, 753 molecules), aqueous solubility (ESOL, 1,128 molecules), and hydration free energy (SAMPL, 642 molecules). The baseline model achieves an average $R^2 = 0.24$, confirming limited transferability. To address this, a systematic enhancement framework is proposed, progressively incorporating Ridge regularization, additional graph descriptors, physicochemical properties, ensemble learning with Gradient Boosting, Lasso feature selection, and a hybrid approach combining topological indices with Morgan fingerprints. The enhanced models raise the average best $R^2$ to 0.79, with individual improvements ranging from 165\% to 274\%. All improvements are statistically significant ($p < 0.001$). A direct comparison with a Graph Convolutional Network under identical experimental conditions shows that the enhanced classical models match or outperform deep learning on all five datasets. Comparison with the recent GNN+PGM hybrid of Djagba et al.\ further confirms competitiveness, with the enhanced models achieving the best results on two datasets and tying on one. The entire framework requires no GPU, trains in under five minutes, and uses only open-source tools, making it accessible for researchers in resource-limited settings.

</details>


### [19] [Rethinking Reinforcement Fine-Tuning in LVLM: Convergence, Reward Decomposition, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19857)
*Carter Adams,Rafael Oliveira,Gabriel Almeida,Sofia Torres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了工具增强马尔可夫决策过程(TA-MDP)框架，从理论层面分析了RLVR范式，证明了GRPO算法的收敛速率、奖励分解定理以及泛化界，解释了视觉智能体强化微调的成功机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于可验证奖励的强化微调(RLVR)范式在赋能大型视觉语言模型方面取得了显著成功，特别是Visual-ARFT，但其理论基础尚不明确。两个关键问题缺乏严格答案：(i)可验证奖励的组合结构如何影响GRPO的收敛性；(ii)为何在少量工具增强任务上的训练能够泛化到分布外领域。

Method: 引入工具增强马尔可夫决策过程(TA-MDP)这一形式化框架，用于建模具有有界深度工具调用的多模态智能体决策过程。在此框架下，针对复合可验证奖励分析GRPO算法，推导奖励分解定理，并建立PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 定理1证明了GRPO在复合可验证奖励下以速率O(1/√T)收敛到一阶驻点，收敛性显式依赖于奖励组件数量和组大小；定理2提出了奖励分解定理，界定了分解优化与联合优化之间的次优性差距，精确刻画了奖励分解何时有益；定理3建立了工具增强策略的PAC-Bayes泛化界，解释了Visual-ARFT中观察到的强分布外迁移能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过TA-MDP框架为RLVR范式提供了坚实的理论基础，从收敛性分析、奖励分解优化和泛化能力三个维度，系统地解释了视觉智能体强化微调的成功机制，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for equipping large vision-language models (LVLMs) with agentic capabilities such as tool use and multi-step reasoning. Despite striking empirical successes, most notably Visual Agentic Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (Visual-ARFT), the theoretical underpinnings of this paradigm remain poorly understood. In particular, two critical questions lack rigorous answers: (i)~how does the composite structure of verifiable rewards (format compliance, answer accuracy, tool executability) affect the convergence of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and (ii)~why does training on a small set of tool-augmented tasks transfer to out-of-distribution domains? We address these gaps by introducing the \emph{Tool-Augmented Markov Decision Process} (TA-MDP), a formal framework that models multimodal agentic decision-making with bounded-depth tool calls. Within this framework, we establish three main results. First, we prove that GRPO under composite verifiable rewards converges to a first-order stationary point at rate $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ with explicit dependence on the number of reward components and group size (\textbf{Theorem~1}). Second, we derive a \emph{Reward Decomposition Theorem} that bounds the sub-optimality gap between decomposed per-component optimization and joint optimization, providing a precise characterization of when reward decomposition is beneficial (\textbf{Theorem~2}). Third, we establish a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for tool-augmented policies that explains the strong out-of-distribution transfer observed in Visual-ARFT (\textbf{Theorem~3}).

</details>

*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani,Kamal Kant Hiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在云边缘环境下融合联邦学习与区块链技术的方案，提出四维架构分类法，比较分析了MORFLB和FBCI-SHS两个前沿框架，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网设备和云计算的普及，传统集中式机器学习模型面临数据泄露、隐私侵犯和监管合规等问题，亟需一种能够保护数据隐私并符合法规要求的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个详细的四维架构分类方法，系统评估了协调框架、共识算法、数据存储实践和信任模型；对两个前沿框架（MORFLB和FBCI-SHS）进行了全面的比较分析。

Result: 展示了联邦学习与区块链技术在云边缘环境中融合的有效性，揭示了MORFLB和FBCI-SHS框架的独特贡献和固有局限性，明确了本研究的独特性。

Conclusion: 指出了主要挑战，并为未来研究方向提供了战略框架，强调需要在多个应用领域推进自适应、弹性和标准化的区块链联邦学习（BCFL）系统的发展。

Abstract: The rise of IoT devices and the uptake of cloud computing have informed a new era of data-driven intelligence. Traditional centralized machine learning models that require a large volume of data to be stored in a single location have therefore become more susceptible to data breaches, privacy violations, and regulatory non-compliance. This report presents a thorough examination of the merging of Federated Learning (FL) and blockchain technology in a cloud-edge setting, demonstrating it as an effective solution to the stated concerns. We are proposing a detailed four-dimensional architectural categorization that meticulously assesses coordination frameworks, consensus algorithms, data storage practices, and trust models that are significant to these integrated systems. The manuscript presents a comprehensive comparative examination of two cutting-edge frameworks: the Multi-Objectives Reinforcement Federated Learning Blockchain (MORFLB), which is designed for intelligent transportation systems, and the Federated Blockchain-IoT Framework for Sustainable Healthcare Systems (FBCI-SHS), elucidating their distinctive contributions and inherent limitations. Lastly, we engage in a thorough evaluation of the literature that integrates a comparative perspective on current frameworks to discern the singular nature of this research within existing knowledge systems. The manuscript culminates in delineating the principal challenges and offering a strategic framework for prospective research trajectories, emphasizing the advancement of adaptive, resilient, and standardized BCFL systems across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [21] [DR-Venus: Towards Frontier Edge-Scale Deep Research Agents with Only 10K Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19859)
*Venus Team,Sunhao Dai,Yong Deng,Jinzhen Lin,Yusheng Song,Guoqing Wang,Xiaofeng Wu,Yuqi Zhou,Shuo Yang,Zhenzhe Ying,Zhanwei Zhang,Changhua Meng,Weiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DR-Venus，一个基于完全开放数据训练的4B参数边缘级深度研究智能体。通过两阶段训练（智能体监督微调+强化学习），在仅约1万条开放数据上，显著优于此前9B以下参数的智能体模型，并缩小了与30B级系统的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘级深度研究智能体在成本、延迟和隐私方面具有优势，但在有限开放数据条件下如何训练出强大的小型智能体是一个关键挑战，需同时提升数据质量和数据利用率。

Method: 采用两阶段训练方案：第一阶段通过智能体监督微调（SFT），结合严格数据清洗和长视野轨迹重采样来提升数据质量与利用率；第二阶段应用智能体强化学习（RL），基于IGPO设计信息增益和格式感知正则化的回合级奖励，以增强监督密度和回合级信用分配。

Result: 在约1万条开放数据上训练的DR-Venus-4B在多个深度研究基准测试中显著优于此前9B以下参数的智能体模型，并缩小了与30B级系统的性能差距；分析显示4B智能体已具备强大的性能潜力。

Conclusion: 小模型（4B）在深度研究任务中已展现出强大的性能潜力，证明了边缘级部署的可行性和测试时扩展的价值。作者发布了模型、代码和关键配方以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Edge-scale deep research agents based on small language models are attractive for real-world deployment due to their advantages in cost, latency, and privacy. In this work, we study how to train a strong small deep research agent under limited open-data by improving both data quality and data utilization. We present DR-Venus, a frontier 4B deep research agent for edge-scale deployment, built entirely on open data. Our training recipe consists of two stages. In the first stage, we use agentic supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to establish basic agentic capability, combining strict data cleaning with resampling of long-horizon trajectories to improve data quality and utilization. In the second stage, we apply agentic reinforcement learning (RL) to further improve execution reliability on long-horizon deep research tasks. To make RL effective for small agents in this setting, we build on IGPO and design turn-level rewards based on information gain and format-aware regularization, thereby enhancing supervision density and turn-level credit assignment. Built entirely on roughly 10K open-data, DR-Venus-4B significantly outperforms prior agentic models under 9B parameters on multiple deep research benchmarks, while also narrowing the gap to much larger 30B-class systems. Our further analysis shows that 4B agents already possess surprisingly strong performance potential, highlighting both the deployment promise of small models and the value of test-time scaling in this setting. We release our models, code, and key recipes to support reproducible research on edge-scale deep research agents.

</details>


### [22] [A Delta-Aware Orchestration Framework for Scalable Multi-Agent Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20129)
*Samaresh Kumar Singh,Joyjit Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAOEF框架，通过同时解决动作空间爆炸、计算冗余和硬件调度三个相互放大因素，解决了超100智能体规模下性能超线性下降的协同崩溃问题，在200智能体云部署中实现62%延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到"协同崩溃"现象：当智能体规模超过100时性能呈超线性下降（150摄像头智能城市部署中截止时间满足率从78%降至34%，年成本超支18万美元）。现有工作孤立解决了指数级动作空间增长、空间相邻智能体间的计算冗余、任务无关硬件调度等单一因素，但未研究这三个因素如何相互作用并相互放大。

Method: 提出DAOEF（面向边缘联合体的增量感知编排）框架，同时处理三个因素：（1）差异神经缓存：存储中间层激活并仅计算输入增量，通过经验校准的相似性阈值，在2%精度损失内实现72%命中率（比输出级缓存高2.1倍）；（2）关键性动作空间剪枝：将智能体组织为优先层级，将协调复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n log n)，最优性损失小于6%；（3）学习型硬件亲和性匹配：将任务分配到最优加速器（GPU/CPU/NPU/FPGA），防止复合不匹配惩罚。

Result: 控制性因素隔离实验表明每个机制必要但不充分：移除任一机制延迟增加超40%，验证收益相互依赖而非叠加。在四个数据集（100-250智能体）和20设备物理测试平台上，DAOEF相比独立应用三个机制获得1.45倍乘性增益。200智能体云部署实现62%延迟降低（280ms vs 735ms），在250智能体规模内保持亚线性延迟增长。

Conclusion: 三个因素（动作空间爆炸、计算冗余、硬件调度）存在协同放大效应，必须同时解决才能有效缓解协同崩溃。DAOEF框架通过联合优化实现超越单一机制简单叠加的效果，为大规模多智能体边缘计算系统提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The Synergistic Collapse occurs when scaling beyond 100 agents causes superlinear performance degradation that individual optimizations cannot prevent. We observe this collapse with 150 cameras in Smart City deployment using MADDPG, where Deadline Satisfaction drops from 78% to 34%, producing approximately $180,000 in annual cost overruns. Prior work has addressed each contributing factor in isolation: exponential action-space growth, computational redundancy among spatially adjacent agents, and task-agnostic hardware scheduling. None has examined how these three factors interact and amplify each other. We present DAOEF (Delta-Aware Orchestration for Edge Federations), a framework that addresses all three simultaneously through: (1) Differential Neural Caching, which stores intermediate layer activations and computes only the input deltas, achieving 2.1x higher hit ratios (72% vs. 35%) than output-level caching while staying within 2% accuracy loss through empirically calibrated similarity thresholds; (2) Criticality-Based Action Space Pruning, which organizes agents into priority tiers and reduces coordination complexity from O(n2) to O(n log n) with less than 6% optimality loss; and (3) Learned Hardware Affinity Matching, which assigns tasks to their optimal accelerator (GPU, CPU, NPU, or FPGA) to prevent compounding mismatch penalties. Controlled factor-isolation experiments confirm that each mechanism is necessary but insufficient on its own: removing any single mechanism increases latency by more than 40%, validating that the gains are interdependent rather than additive. Across four datasets (100-250 agents) and a 20-device physical testbed, DAOEF achieves a 1.45x multiplicative gain over applying the three mechanisms independently. A 200-agent cloud deployment yields 62% latency reduction (280 ms vs. 735 ms), sub-linear latency growth up to 250 agents.

</details>


### [23] [Super Apriel: One Checkpoint, Many Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19877)
*SLAM Labs,:,Oleksiy Ostapenko,Raymond Li,Torsten Scholak,Alireza Mousavi-Hosseini,Aman Tiwari,Denis Kocetkov,Joel Lamy Poirier,Kelechi Ogueji,Nanda H Krishna,Rafael Pardinas,Sathwik Tejaswi Madhusudhan,Shruthan Radhakrishna,Srinivas Sunkara,Valerie Becaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发布Super Apriel，一个15B参数的超网络，支持四种混合器选择，可在服务时动态切换配置以平衡速度与质量，解码吞吐量提升2.9×至10.7×，并开源权重与工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在推理时难以灵活权衡速度与质量的问题，以及传统方法需要部署多个独立模型或草稿模型带来的资源开销，旨在通过单一检查点实现多档速度预设和高效推测解码。

Method: 构建每层提供四种混合器选择（全注意力FA、滑动窗注意力SWA、Kimi Delta Attention KDA、Gated DeltaNet GDN）的15B超网络，通过随机蒸馏从冻结的Apriel 1.6教师模型训练，再经监督微调；使用代理模型预测配置质量以导航巨大配置空间，并研究不同规模下配置排名的稳定性。

Result: 全FA预设与教师模型性能持平；推荐混合预设在96%至77%质量保留下实现2.9×至10.7×解码吞吐量提升，长上下文优势更明显；0.5B规模下排名快速稳定，但15B规模最高效配置更不稳定。

Conclusion: Super Apriel通过共享权重超网络实现了单一检查点多速度预设，显著提升了推理效率，且支持无需额外草稿模型的推测解码；但从小模型外推最优配置需谨慎。

Abstract: We release Super Apriel, a 15B-parameter supernet in which every decoder layer provides four trained mixer choices -- Full Attention (FA), Sliding Window Attention (SWA), Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), and Gated DeltaNet (GDN). A placement selects one mixer per layer; placements can be switched between requests at serving time without reloading weights, enabling multiple speed presets from a single checkpoint. The shared checkpoint also enables speculative decoding without a separate draft model. The all-FA preset matches the Apriel 1.6 teacher on all reported benchmarks; recommended hybrid presets span $2.9\times$ to $10.7\times$ decode throughput at 96% to 77% quality retention, with throughput advantages that compound at longer context lengths. With four mixer types across 48 layers, the configuration space is vast. A surrogate that predicts placement quality from the per-layer mixer assignment makes the speed-quality landscape tractable and identifies the best tradeoffs at each speed level. We investigate whether the best configurations at each speed level can be identified early in training or only after convergence. Rankings stabilize quickly at 0.5B scale, but the most efficient configurations exhibit higher instability at 15B, cautioning against extrapolation from smaller models. Super Apriel is trained by stochastic distillation from a frozen Apriel 1.6 teacher, followed by supervised fine-tuning. We release the supernet weights, Fast-LLM training code, vLLM serving code, and a placement optimization toolkit.

</details>


### [24] [Stream-CQSA: Avoiding Out-of-Memory in Attention Computation via Flexible Workload Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20819)
*Yiming Bian,Joshua M. Akey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQS Divide操作和Stream-CQSA框架，将注意力机制分解为独立子序列计算，实现精确的内存自适应调度，使单GPU能处理十亿token序列的精确注意力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型的扩展性受限于精确自注意力机制的二次方内存开销，现有方法虽改善了内存效率但仍假设完整Q、K、V张量能装入设备内存，无法处理超长序列。

Method: 基于循环仲裁集(CQS)理论提出CQS Divide操作，将注意力分解为独立子序列计算，重组后得到与全序列精确相同的结果；在此基础上构建Stream-CQSA内存自适应调度框架，将注意力划分为适配任意内存预算的子问题集合。

Result: 实验表明该方法具有可预测的内存扩展性，在单GPU上通过流式处理成功执行十亿token序列的精确注意力计算，无需修改注意力的数学定义或引入近似误差。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力从逻辑上的整体操作转化为可调度任务集合，突破了长上下文模型的内存限制，为精确注意力的大规模应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The scalability of long-context large language models is fundamentally limited by the quadratic memory cost of exact self-attention, which often leads to out-of-memory (OOM) failures on modern hardware. Existing methods improve memory efficiency to near-linear complexity, while assuming that the full query, key, and value tensors fit in device memory. In this work, we remove this assumption by introducing CQS Divide, an operation derived from cyclic quorum sets (CQS) theory that decomposes attention into a set of independent subsequence computations whose recomposition yields exactly the same result as full-sequence attention. Exploiting this decomposition, we introduce Stream-CQSA, a memory-adaptive scheduling framework that partitions attention into subproblems that fit within arbitrary memory budgets. This recasts attention from a logically monolithic operation into a collection of schedulable tasks, enabling flexible execution across devices without inter-device communication. Experiments demonstrate predictable memory scaling and show that exact attention over billion-token sequences can be executed on a single GPU via streaming, without changing the underlying mathematical definition of attention or introducing approximation error.

</details>


### [25] [A Multi-Plant Machine Learning Framework for Emission Prediction, Forecasting, and Control in Cement Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19903)
*Sheikh Junaid Fayaz,Nestor D. Montiel-Bohorquez,Wilson Ricardo Leal da Silva,Shashank Bishnoi,Matteo Romano,Manuele Gatti,N. M. Anoop Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了数据驱动的NOx排放控制框架，通过机器学习预测和早期预警，可在不增加硬件的情况下降低34-64%的NOx排放并节省约58,000美元/年的氨水成本，为水泥等难减排行业提供了通用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水泥生产是工业空气污染的主要来源之一，每年排放约300万吨NOx。行业标准的非选择性催化还原（SNCR）技术氨水利用效率低，导致运行效率低下和试剂成本增加。亟需一种更高效的排放控制方法来减少NOx排放并降低运营成本。

Method: 研究团队开发了数据驱动的排放控制框架，利用全球四家水泥工厂的大规模运行数据，基准测试了九种机器学习架构。方法的核心包括：1）引入短期过程历史数据以捕捉NOx形成的过程记忆特性；2）开发能够提前9分钟预测NOx超调的预测模型；3）建立代理模型进行排放控制策略优化，在上游控制NOx形成而非仅在下游处理。

Result: 研究发现：1）预测误差因数据丰富度差异在不同工厂间存在3-5倍的变异；2）引入短期过程历史使NOx预测精度提高近三倍，揭示了NOx形成具有显著的过程记忆和时间尺度依赖性（不同于CO和CO2）；3）模型能够提前9分钟预测NOx超调，为运行调整提供缓冲时间；4）代理模型预测可减少34-64%的NOx排放（约290吨NOx/年），同时保持熟料质量，节省约58,000美元/年的氨水成本。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个可推广的数据驱动排放控制框架，实现了在不进行结构改造或增加硬件的情况下的低排放运行。该方法通过在源头控制NOx形成而非仅在下游SNCR处理，为水泥行业提供了经济有效的减排路径，并具有推广到钢铁、玻璃和石灰等其他难减排行业的潜力。

Abstract: Cement production is among the largest contributors to industrial air pollution, emitting ~3 Mt NOx/year. The industry-standard mitigation approach, selective non-catalytic reduction (SNCR), exhibits low NH3 utilization efficiency, resulting in operational inefficiencies and increased reagent costs. Here, we develop a data-driven framework for emission control using large-scale operational data from four cement plants worldwide. Benchmarking nine machine learning architectures, we observe that prediction error varies ~3-5x across plants due to variation in data richness. Incorporating short-term process history nearly triples NOx prediction accuracy, revealing that NOx formation carries substantial process memory, a timescale dependence that is absent in CO and CO2. Further, we develop models that forecast NOx overshoots as early as nine minutes, providing a buffer for operational adjustments. The developed framework controls NOx formation at the source, reducing NH3 consumption in downstream SNCR. Surrogate model projections estimate a ~34-64% reduction in NOx while preserving clinker quality, corresponding to a reduction of ~290 t NOx/year and ~58,000 USD/year in NH3 savings. This work establishes a generalizable framework for data-driven emission control, offering a pathway toward low-emission operation without structural modifications or additional hardware, with potential applicability to other hard-to-abate industries such as steel, glass, and lime.

</details>


### [26] [FedSIR: Spectral Client Identification and Relabeling for Federated Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20825)
*Sina Gholami,Abdulmoneam Ali,Tania Haghighi,Ahmed Arafa,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSIR框架，通过利用客户端特征表示的谱结构来识别和缓解联邦学习中的标签噪声问题，在标准基准测试上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许在不共享原始数据的情况下进行协作模型训练，但分布式客户端中存在的标签噪声会严重降低学习性能。现有的方法主要依赖于设计抗噪损失函数或利用训练期间的损失动态，效果有限。

Method: 提出FedSIR多阶段框架：1）通过分析类特征子空间的谱一致性来识别干净客户端和噪声客户端；2）干净客户端提供谱参考，使噪声客户端能够利用主导类方向和残差子空间重新标记潜在损坏的样本；3）采用噪声感知训练策略，集成logit调整损失、知识蒸馏和距离感知聚合来进一步稳定联邦优化。

Result: 在标准联邦学习基准测试上的大量实验表明，FedSIR在处理标签噪声方面始终优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedSIR成功地将谱分析方法引入联邦学习的标签噪声问题，通过谱结构识别和缓解标签噪声，为联邦学习在现实噪声数据场景下的应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data; however, the presence of noisy labels across distributed clients can severely degrade the learning performance. In this paper, we propose FedSIR, a multi-stage framework for robust FL under noisy labels. Different from existing approaches that mainly rely on designing noise-tolerant loss functions or exploiting loss dynamics during training, our method leverages the spectral structure of client feature representations to identify and mitigate label noise.
  Our framework consists of three key components. First, we identify clean and noisy clients by analyzing the spectral consistency of class-wise feature subspaces with minimal communication overhead. Second, clean clients provide spectral references that enable noisy clients to relabel potentially corrupted samples using both dominant class directions and residual subspaces. Third, we employ a noise-aware training strategy that integrates logit-adjusted loss, knowledge distillation, and distance-aware aggregation to further stabilize federated optimization. Extensive experiments on standard FL benchmarks demonstrate that FedSIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods for FL with noisy labels. The code is available at https://github.com/sinagh72/FedSIR.

</details>

*Huy Hoang Le,Haoguang Wang,Christian Moya,Marcos Netto,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种扩展牛顿隐式层方法，结合物理信息DeepONet，解决了刚性微分代数方程（DAE）神经网络代理模型中代数约束和刚性的关键挑战，显著降低了误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性微分代数方程的神经代理模型面临两大挑战：软约束方法会因刚性放大代数残差导致大误差，而硬约束方法需要昂贵的刚性积分器生成的轨迹数据，限制了模型性能与效率。

Method: 引入扩展牛顿隐式层，在单次可微分求解中强制代数一致性和准稳态约简；利用物理信息DeepONet预测慢状态，通过隐式层恢复快状态与代数状态；基于隐函数定理的梯度捕捉刚度尺度耦合项；采用级联隐式层扩展至多组件系统。

Result: 在21状态电网形成逆变器DAE系统上，输出7个变量，误差仅1.42%，显著优于惩罚方法（39.3%）和标准牛顿方法（57.0%）；两个独立训练的模型无需重训即可组合成44状态系统，误差0.72-1.16%，代数残差为零；共形预测在分布内达到90%覆盖率并实现分布外检测。

Conclusion: 该方法有效消除了刚性放大误差路径，实现了高效的代数一致性与降维，具备良好的可组合性和不确定性量化能力，为复杂刚性DAE系统提供了高性能神经代理解决方案。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for stiff differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) face two key challenges: soft-constraint methods leave algebraic residuals that stiffness amplifies into large errors, while hard-constraint methods require trajectory data from computationally expensive stiff integrators. We introduce an extended Newton implicit layer that enforces algebraic consistency and quasi-steady-state reduction within a single differentiable solve. Given slow-state predictions from a physics-informed DeepONet, the proposed layer recovers fast and algebraic states, eliminates the stiffness-amplification pathway within each time window, and reduces the output dimension to the slow states alone. Gradients derived via the implicit function theorem capture a stiffness-scaled coupling term that is absent in penalty-based approaches. Cascaded implicit layers further extend the framework to multi-component systems with provable convergence. On a grid-forming inverter DAE (21 states), the proposed method (7 outputs, 1.42 percent error) significantly outperforms penalty methods (39.3 percent), standard Newton approaches (57.0 percent), and augmented Lagrangian or feedback linearization baselines, which fail to converge. Two independently trained models compose into a 44-state system without retraining, achieving 0.72 to 1.16 percent error with zero algebraic residual. Conformal prediction further provides 90 percent coverage in-distribution and enables automatic out-of-distribution detection.

</details>
